# ForgeOS — Roadmap

> 纪律:每一版可独立验证;不把 ForgeOS 自己做成上帝项目。

## v0 — 止血 (✅ 完成)
- [x] `.agent/` 文档骨架(愿景归档)
- [x] `harness/gate.mjs`:行数 + 根目录数闸门(host-independent,`enforce: block`,8 node:test)
- [x] `refactor-large-file` / `project-reorganization` skills
- [x] `BOOTSTRAP.md` + `.agent/project.yml`(engineering/mvp)+ 2 ADR + `.gitignore`
- [x] 函数行数 / 循环依赖执法:已由 `arch-check`(zero-dep 启发式 parser,8 检查之二)**机器执法**(`max_function_lines:50` · 循环依赖=0,Sprint 5 dogfood 真抓 113 行测试函数→被迫重构)。〔`harness/adapters/{ts,py,go}.yml` 的外部-linter 路径是**可选冗余**第二实现,待真实违规目标仓接入;arch-check 已覆盖该需求,故本仓接入零增量收益〕
- [x] `.claude/settings.json`:CC PostToolUse 加速器 —— 用户授权后落地(Edit/Write/MultiEdit 后自动跑 `gate.mjs`,FAIL→exit 2 把违规喂回 Claude 即时修;团队共享 git-tracked、可逆;快即时信号、不替代完整 `forge accept`)

## v1 — 闭环 + Claude 档路由 (✅ 完成)
- [x] 13 agent 卡 + 9 skill 卡 + 7 workflow(含独立 deploy/rollback) + mode×lifecycle 矩阵 + 路由策略 + 评估 schema
- [x] `forge check` 资产校验器(`harness/check.py`,当前 13 项治理检查，含 Agent Engineering v1 聚合校验)
- [x] 验证脊柱已跑通:plan→implementer×2→gate→reviewer(fresh)→fix 全 spine;workflow↔角色卡 SoT 漂移已消除
- [x] `forge accept` —— acceptance Stop 闸门(`harness/acceptance.mjs` 聚合 11 类判据，递归发现 harness tests，并对 Go/Node/Python/Rust/Java 项目要求可观察的正测试数；**n/a 项诚实可见,绝不伪造通过**)
- [x] **Dogfood:首个真实应用 `examples/url-shortener` 经完整 pipeline(architect→3 implementer→fresh reviewer→fix)端到端建成并保持套件全绿,被 `forge accept` 实际 gate**;reviewer 揪出"app 未被 accept 覆盖"治理洞并已补。**Build→Review→Accept 脊柱在真实产品上验证成立。**
- [x] 真·无人值守闭环驱动 → 闭环引擎已建(`forge-core` `forge evolve`:phases→gate→converge→loop,带 max-iter/no-progress tripwire)**且真·活体 agent 端到端已接通坐实**(Sprint 24-26:真 `--agent-cmd=claude` 多-agent 跑到 converge MET、增量+版本级,八个真跑 gap 全修,见 CURRENT_SPRINT + docs/ignition.md)
- [x] **Strict Build QA handshake(ADR 0014)**:`verdict_contract: qa_v1` 不可被 mode 跳过，强制在所有 mode 实跑独立 `test` gate 并回到更早、可写、不可跳过的 `implementer`，只接受精确最终非空行 `QA_VERDICT: ACCEPTED|REJECTED`;缺失/畸形、无法回流及 loop budget 耗尽均失败关闭，普通 Reviewer/Evolve/Release 契约不变
- [x] **Evidence-backed Evolve scan contract(ADR 0020)**:首 phase 显式声明 `scan_contract: evolve_scan_v1`，把有效 mode×lifecycle depth 冻结进提示、裁决与完整 canonical feed-forward；六维扫描只接受真实仓内 UTF-8 regular-file 证据，thorough 强制全维且 finding 自动派生 candidate task。checkpoint v3 以整数微美元、Agent-call 与 loop-back 资源信封持久恢复；串行已完成 scan 不重放，parallel 只诚实承诺 iteration-boundary 恢复，旧 workflow 保持兼容

## v2 — 全局化 + 学习闭环 (forge-core 已落地,持续推进)
**forge-core 已存在、已构建、全绿**：绝大多数 Go 包保持标准库；R0 `internal/controlstore` 为 SQLite 明确固定 `modernc.org/sqlite v1.57.0` 及 `go.sum` 依赖闭包。CLI 已覆盖 `run/evolve/init/trace/approve/reject/preflight/doctor/gate/check/accept` 等；`forge run --chain` 持久化跨阶段状态并执行 Discover→Design→Review→Build→Deploy→Evolve，`forge evolve` 按真实 stop signal 收敛。产物 JSONL v1 记录 run/workflow/phase/agent/model/hash，planner/审批/拒绝/CTO halt 均有机器契约；Rust/Java adapter 与 init/upgrade copy-anywhere 已接入验收。
- **DONE — Wave 3–A1 Proposed-only ADR v2**:ADR 0067 为新建 Proposed ADR 冻结 exact canonical JSON frontmatter、固定 Markdown body、声明式 owner/approver/Claim/Evidence/affected-node/validation/revisit 字段与 body/self digest；Python strict checker、Go current-`writes_adr` candidate validator、physical golden、registry v22、shadow detector、Skill 与 universal scaffold 已接线。Universal checker 不扫描旧 ADR；Go 只为既有 baseline-integrity fingerprint 读取旧字节，但不做旧 ADR 的 v2 parse、retro-validation 或 migration。它不认证身份/ApprovalRecord/SoD，不解析引用或产出 Graph coverage，不接受决策，也不实现 immutability、supersession/compliance、persistence 或 lifecycle effect。
- **DONE — Wave 4–A1 Authority-neutral Capability Registry v1 evaluator**:ADR 0068 的 Proposed 状态与 wire `staged` 状态保持不变；Go/Python strict validator/resolver、显式输入 CLI、physical checker、exact golden、registry v23、shadow detector、Skill 和 universal scaffold 已接线。唯一 entry 是 `local-go-package-impact-prescan/1`；140-item planning catalog projection、catalog→package adapter generation、CapabilityInvocation、Grant/PDP、plugin lifecycle 与 runtime routing 全部保持未交付。
- **DONE — Wave 4–A2 Planning Capability Ownership Projection v1**:ADR 0069 继续为 Proposed；Python/Go strict pure projector、exact golden、`forge capability-ownership project --catalog FILE|- --mapping FILE|-`、registry v24、shadow detector、governance 与 source-only universal scaffold 已接线。只关闭 140 unique fine capabilities → 38 declared package primary owners 的完整唯一覆盖及 unresolved logical adapter refs；38 package/physical Skill/host adapter 实现、cross-reference runtime、ADR-0068 Registry mutation、CapabilityInvocation、Grant/PDP、plugin lifecycle 与 runtime routing 均保持未交付。
- **DONE — Wave 4–B1 `project-snapshot` narrow package slice**:ADR 0070 继续为 Proposed；Linux-only Go live producer/CLI、strict Python checker/golden、closed portable Skill、`.agent` adapter、shadow detector、registry v25 与 fresh/legacy scaffold 已接线。它只关闭 38-package parent 下的 `project-snapshot` nested item；观察保持 bounded interval、non-atomic、coverage partial/unknown，path policy 不证明 secret absence，scaffold 不复制 runtime/安装 Skill/授予权限，Graph/Registry mutation/Grant/PDP/CapabilityInvocation/routing/persistence/effect 仍未交付。
- **DONE — Wave 4–B2 `context-engineering` narrow package slice**:ADR 0071 继续为 Proposed；closed portable Skill、零参数 exact-stdin adapter、strict manifest checker、shadow detector、registry v26 与 fresh/legacy scaffold 已接线。它只包装 ADR-0055 已冻结的 supplied-bytes pure projection，并关闭 parent 下的 context-engineering narrow package slice；不交付 live source/provider/model/tokenizer/prompt、host installation、Grant/PDP/Approval、truth/instruction/persistence/routing/effect authority，parent 与其余 36 package items 仍开放。
- **DONE — Wave 4–B3 `evidence-claim-management` narrow package slice**:ADR 0072 继续为 Proposed；closed 18-file portable Skill、零参数 explicit-EOF exact-stdin validator、strict manifest checker、shadow detector、registry v27 与 source-only fresh/legacy scaffold 已接线。它只包装 ADR-0045 的 already-authored record-set structural validation，不交付 observation/authorship/repair/journal/semantic view/proposal、host installation、truth/instruction/Grant/PDP/Approval/persistence/routing/transition/execution/effect authority，parent 与其余 35 package items 仍开放。
- **DONE — Wave 4–B4 `policy-authority` narrow package slice**:ADR 0073 继续为 Proposed；closed 30-file portable Skill、两个独立零参数 explicit-EOF exact-stdin declared-assessment adapter、strict manifest checker、shadow detector、registry v28 与 source-only fresh/legacy scaffold 已接线。它只包装 ADR-0056/0059 frozen pure evaluators，不交付 combined envelope、issuance/effective Approval、live policy/identity/revocation/usage、Kernel/PDP/PEP、host installation、authorization/persistence/routing/transition/execution/effect authority，parent 与其余 34 package items 保持开放。
- **DONE — Wave 4–B5 `adr-governance` narrow package slice**:ADR 0074 继续为 Proposed；closed 25-file portable Skill、exactly-one-basename-argument explicit-EOF exact-stdin validator、strict manifest checker、shadow detector、registry v29 与 source-only fresh/legacy scaffold 已接线。它只包装 ADR-0067 frozen Proposed-document validator；caller basename 不证明 physical identity，不交付 new envelope、repository scan、authoring/repair/reseal、Accepted lifecycle/compliance、DECISIONS query、legacy import、Go `writes_adr` runtime、host installation、persistence/execution/effect authority，parent 与其余 33 package items 保持开放。
- [x] **Rust Agent Runtime 首切片(ADR 0006)**:`forge-runtime/` clean-room 实现单一模型/工具循环、版本化 JSONL 事件、turn/tool/output 硬上限、能力检查、workspace 内只读工具与 deterministic provider；全程离线,不调用真实 LLM。
- [x] **Local-first Conversation Hub(ADR 0007 + 0009)**:CLI 无路径进入 Global、有路径/`-C` 进入 Project、`--group` 进入 Group；SQLite 跨进程持久化 Project/Conversation/Prompt 与 frontend/backend/SSO 等带角色标签的联动。`group context` 能在单一 SQLite 快照中生成有界、因果有序、带来源和 SHA-256 摘要的 Group/member-Project Prompt dossier，默认不显示正文且绝不读取项目文件；`group run prepare/show/list` 进一步把 exact dossier 原子冻结为独立 prepared Group Run，同幂等键重放旧字节而不查询最新历史，也不启动 provider/tool/workspace。Prompt 仍由调用者显式追加；Project Run 会以所选 user Prompt 为边界注入严格 512 KiB 的既有 user/assistant 历史。远程账号/同步、共享 ACL 与多 Agent 组执行继续分期。
- [x] **Local Group execution integrity receipt(ADR 0010)**:`group execution start/show/list` 把已验证 prepared Group Run 绑定到独立 SQLite v4 execution，持久化有界三事件证据与 content-free receipt；同 key 跨进程重放终态，或仅为合法 incomplete 前缀补确定性 suffix。该同步管理路径不构造模型/provider/runtime/workspace/tool/network，不创建 Project Run/assistant Prompt，也不把 integrity validation 冒充分析、讨论或任务结果；单模型消费由下项 ADR 0011 的独立边界接续，multi-Agent execution 仍属后续。
- [x] **Two-phase Group model analysis(ADR 0011)**:`group analysis prepare/show/list/send` 在 SQLite v5 中把一份已验证 frozen dossier 绑定到精确、零工具、`store:false` 的 Responses 请求。prepare 纯本地且原子；send 要求当次显式外发同意并在 claim 前完成凭证/目标预检，只有 claim 赢家取得 exact body 并至多发送一次。claim 后任何不确定错误都禁止自动重发；仅真实完整 terminal 可原子落库，默认输出隐藏请求、事件和结果正文。它是单模型分析，不冒充 multi-Agent 讨论、事实认证、工具执行或 Conversation memory。
- [x] **Durable local Group analysis panel(ADR 0013)**:`group panel prepare/show/list` 在 SQLite v6 中把同一 exact Group Run 的 2–8 份 terminal-completed 分析按调用者顺序冻结为 canonical manifest；同 key 精确重放，变更来源、结果或顺序即冲突，`length` 不可入组。默认隐藏结果正文，显式查看也会重验 Group Run、源分析、member rows、canonical bytes 与 digest；整个路径无 provider/凭证/网络/workspace/tool/Conversation/task/memory 副作用，且只声明本地并排组装，不冒充讨论、综合或共识。
- [x] **Two-phase Group panel synthesis(ADR 0015)**:`group synthesis prepare/send/show/list` 把一个 exact panel 的 canonical manifest 固定为唯一 user message，原子持久化零工具、`store:false` 的 exact request；每次 send 都需新的 off-machine consent，claim 前重验 panel/源结果/目标/字节，只有单赢家获得 request authority，claim 后不自动重发。结果诚实标记为 single-model synthesis，不冒充讨论/共识/事实验证，也不访问 dossier/workspace/tool 或写回 Conversation/task/memory。
- [x] **Durable local Group Agent Graph(ADR 0017)**:`group graph prepare/show/list` 把一个 exact frozen Group Run、manager 标签/指令、1–32 个绑定 frozen `project_id+role` 的 Agent task、dependency edges 与 authored-order deterministic waves 冻结为 SQLite v8 immutable manifest。同 key 只重放完全相同的 semantic graph，默认隐藏 spec，显式查看会重验 source/member/canonical bytes/digest；本切片只生成执行图，不运行 manager/node、不访问 provider/network/tool、不扫描 member workspace（只可读取 caller 明示的 spec file），也不写回 task/memory。
- [x] **Core-owned passive Graph Run plan(ADR 0018)**:`forge graph-plan` 把 workflow 与 Group Graph 收敛到同一个 authored-order dependency 实现，并输出跨 Go/Rust 共用 golden 锁定的 canonical Core Plan。`group graph run prepare/show/list` 在 SQLite v9 被动校验并冻结 exact Graph/manifest/plan 与唯一 preparation event；状态固定 `awaiting_execution_contract`，execution-contract/dispatch-authority 永远为 false。本切片不选择 next wave、不 claim node、不运行 manager/node/model/tool/workspace，也不产生 result/writeback。
- [x] **Core-owned first-node execution contract(ADR 0019)**:`group graph run control export` 输出 exact private v1 control snapshot；`forge graph-node-contract` 唯一选择 `plan.waves[0][0]`，冻结 Prompt、provider/model、预算、零 workspace/tool/dataflow 与 no-retry policy；`contract admit/show/list` 在 SQLite v10 以 seq/head CAS 原子登记唯一契约并把 Run 推到 `awaiting_core_dispatch`。dispatch authority 仍为 false，未读 credential、未调用 model/provider/Agent、未访问 workspace/network/tool，也未产生 result/writeback。
- [x] **Core-owned Node Dispatch Request preparation(ADR 0021)**:`group graph run dispatch prepare/show/list` 只用现有 Responses 纯 codec，把已 admission 的 first-node logical request 确定性编码并在 SQLite v11 原子保存 exact bytes、content-addressed identity 与 seq-3 receipt；Run 停在 v3 `awaiting_dispatch_authorization`。默认输出隐藏 body/Prompt/endpoint/model/pricing，只有显式 `--include-request` 揭示 exact body；整个路径不收集 consent、不读 credential、不构造 provider/transport、不访问 network/workspace/tool，也不产生 result/writeback 或调度后继。
- [x] **Effect-free Node Dispatch release authorization(ADR 0022)**:`dispatch release-control export` 从完整重验的 v3 source/contract/seq-3 head/exact request 导出 private canonical snapshot；`forge graph-node-dispatch-authorize` 由 Go 独立重建 base control、request 与全部 scheduler binding，并生成绑定 exact bytes/head/lane/budget/failure policy 的 content-addressed authorization；Rust `dispatch authorization verify` 再对当前 durable state 独立复验。schema 仍为 v11、Run 仍为 v3，authorization 不落库且 authority/consent/credential/provider/network/lane/result/writeback/advance 均保持 false。
- [x] **Effect-free registered destination and pricing readiness(ADR 0023)**:`forge graph-node-pricing-snapshot` 生成固定官方 Responses destination 的 canonical `operator_asserted` 定价 artifact；Rust 以相同整数向上取整算法、authorization binding 与 frozen budget 复验 exact bytes，`dispatch readiness verify` 只读 current Hub 并保持 consent/credential/provider/network/lane/result/database/advance 全部为 false。该 artifact 无 vendor attestation，不冒充实时厂商价格或账单保证。
- [x] **Single-node Dispatch terminal lifecycle(ADR 0024)**:`dispatch execute` 对严格单节点 Graph 重验 current v3 aggregate、fresh consent、authorization/pricing 与固定 provider 后，以 seq-3/head CAS 原子 claim Hub-global Project lane；approved service path 的 non-`Clone` authority 只发送一次 exact body，可信 store adapter 是进程内 TCB。bounded terminal/EOF 或 uncertainty 被固化为 artifact，Linux bridge 从密封并复验后的匿名 memfd 执行 operator-pinned Go Core，对真实 v4 control 生成 receipt；SQLite v12 再原子保存证据、追加 seq 5、释放 lane并进入 completed/failed/failed_uncertain。可捕获的 provider/protocol/timeout 与显式 application cancellation 等失败以 uncertainty 进入 v5；CLI v1 的 OS signal 或其他 hard crash、Core 失败、最终提交不确定性保留 v4 quarantine。所有 claim 后路径均禁止自动重发或 lease 释放；多节点执行仍未开放。
- [x] **Passive multi-node Graph Execution Schedule(ADR 0025)**:`forge graph-execution-schedule` 从 exact private v1 control 冻结 serial wave-then-authored order、Project lane digest、direct-predecessor receipt slots、initial frontier 与 fail-fast/no-dataflow policy；Rust `schedule admit/show/list` 在 SQLite v13 以 immutable sidecar 保存并完整重验。它不占用 Graph 主 journal、不创建 contract、不观察 progress、不推进 successor，且不接触 credential/provider/network/workspace/tool/result/writeback；默认输出隐藏 node/predecessor/lane，single-node 仍走 ADR 0024。真正多节点 contract v2、receipt/result dataflow 与 successor advancement 仍未开放。
- [x] **Passive schedule-bound initial-node contract candidate(ADR 0026)**:`forge graph-scheduled-node-contract` 从 exact private v1 control 独立重建 schedule，只匹配 caller 声明的 digest，不读取 Hub；Rust admission 再要求该 digest 对应已持久化 schedule，并唯一接受 ordinal 0，冻结 pristine seq-1 head、Prompt/provider/budget、空 predecessor/receipt 与 no-workspace/tool/dataflow/retry policy。`scheduled-contract admit/show/list` 在 SQLite v14 保存 immutable v2 sidecar；candidate v2 与 legacy lifecycle contract v1 在 `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` 中互斥，Run/main journal 不变；默认输出隐藏全部 private candidate。candidate 本身不是 lifecycle contract/request/authority/progress/receipt/successor，不能 dispatch，receipt v1 也不能冒充中间 predecessor。
- [x] **Passive scheduled-node provider-request sidecar(ADR 0027)**:`scheduled-contract provider-request prepare/show/list` 只从完整重验的 ordinal-zero candidate 通过确定性纯 codec 生成并保存 exact Responses bytes。SQLite v15 的独立 immutable sidecar 不改变 Run v1/seq-1/main journal，不进入 legacy request/authorization/readiness/claim/execute discovery；默认隐藏 body，只有 `show --include-request` 显式揭示。prepare 不读取 credential、不构造 provider/transport、不联网或访问 workspace，也不授予 lifecycle/dispatch/lane/progress/receipt/successor authority。
- [x] **Effect-free scheduled-node dispatch authorization(ADR 0028)**:Rust 从 current v15 scheduled request 导出 private canonical release control，Go 独立重建 schedule/candidate/request 并生成 content-addressed decision，Rust 再以 fresh state verify。decision 只授权未来 exact lifecycle admission + execution/dispatch release；当前 admitted/released facts、schema v15 与 Run v1/seq-1 均不变，且无 DB write/consent/credential/provider/network/lane/progress/receipt/successor effect。共享 golden、adversarial/current-state 测试、真实跨进程 CLI、完整 gate、双 fresh-context review 与独立 Codex 黑盒 QA 均已通过。
- [x] **Effect-free scheduled-node dispatch readiness(ADR 0029)**:复用 Go 既有 source-neutral canonical pricing snapshot，Rust 对 current scheduled authorization、官方 registered destination、exact pricing bytes、checked component-rounded cost 与 frozen budget 作 fresh read-only 组合验证。schema v15、Run v1/seq-1 与全部 current effect facts 不变；不缓存 readiness，不读 credential、不构造 provider、不联网、不 claim/send/result/receipt/successor。Domain/Application/registry/CLI 对抗测试、真实 Go→Rust E2E、双 fresh review 与独立 Codex 黑盒 QA 均已通过。
- [x] **Independent scheduled-node effectful dispatch lifecycle(ADR 0030)**:scheduled ordinal-zero request 在 fresh authorization/pricing、header-safe credential 与 pinned Go Core preflight 后进入 SQLite v16 独立 sidecar；立即事务原子 claim exact prepared body 与全局 Project lane，bounded provider evidence 经 scheduled terminal control/receipt 校验后再原子 terminalize/release。prepared envelope digest 与 exact body digest 分开绑定；Core/commit failure 只生成不可重发的 artifact-only quarantine。Run 仍 v1/seq-1，legacy lifecycle 不可发现该 sidecar，协议 v1 不开放 retry/resume/lease/health/successor；测试只使用 deterministic/local fixture 与本地 Core。
- [x] **Operator-driven scheduled successor/wave protocol(ADR 0031–0036; SQLite v17–v24)**:每个非空直接前驱都必须由 exact durable `completed`/result 型逐节点逐 attempt receipt 满足；空直接前驱的显式 ordinal>0 target 可在零 receipt 集上就绪。Go 按 schedule 的直接前驱顺序生成 passive per-node successor-v2 candidate，Rust 持久化并完整复验 per-node candidate/request/lifecycle sidecar。可选前驱 result 正文严格限制为 1 MiB，绑定 canonical 第一直接前驱 receipt，并要求独立 disclosure consent；effectful dispatch 仍要求 fresh off-machine consent、Project lane 单赢家与 no-resend quarantine。`ready-nodes`/`wave-admit` 只负责规划/admission，不是顶层整图执行、失败传播/resume 循环，也不承诺 remote exactly-once。v24 仅把 successor candidate 存储上限提高到 8 MiB，initial candidate 仍为 4 MiB。
- [x] **Scheduled Graph Progress Snapshot + Core Reconcile v1（ADR-0096 Proposed；read-only）**:`group graph run reconcile` 在 exact-current SQLite v29 的一个 deferred transaction 中投影整份 schedule 的 content-free durable progress，并通过显式 SHA-256 pinned Go Core 生成 source-bound 七类 disposition。Rust 重验 snapshot/decision canonical bytes、digest 与 source binding；`ready` 只给出观察到的 exact next ordinal/node。Forge Runtime 与已测试的 repository-built official Core 路径无 migration/Hub logical write、credential/workspace/network/provider/lane/consent/dispatch/recovery/retry/resend/successor effect，且不把 non-contiguous wave evidence 冒充 schedule-v1 serial progress。Core 仍是 operator-trusted same-user TCB；pin/handshake 不提供 publisher/function attestation、filesystem/network sandbox 或 effect containment。CLI 以 `effect_facts_scope="forge_runtime"` 约束 `runtime_effect_facts`，另用 `core_trust_boundary` 与 Human TCB 警示显式披露缺少 Core isolation/containment/attestation；这些字段不是 arbitrary child 的 syscall observation。Sprint 137 已用真实 claim→terminalize 交错、32-node count/order、representative snapshots 的 64 KiB 内检查与 42 个独立 stored-corruption states 闭合 pre-effect storage validation；该证据不宣称 byte-maximal snapshot。
- [x] **Core-selected scheduled ready-node release authorization v2（ADR-0097 Proposed；zero-effect）**:`group graph run ready-release` 先从 atomic progress S0 调用既有 pinned reconcile Core 并验证 exact `ready` decision，再以独立 handshake 2 和 v2 wire 将用同一 S0/decision 绑定的 atomic durable bundle A 交给 authorization Core；后者重跑 exact reconcile、只接受同一 `ready` ordinal/node，并重建 selected initial/successor candidate、prepared request、有序直接前驱 receipt closure 与可选首直接前驱 result artifact。Runtime 不跨任一 child 持有 transaction，authorization Core 返回后从第二个 atomic bundle B 要求 A/B source/control exact equality，再验证 metadata-only `maximum_future_node_releases=1` policy。release v1 的 initial/ordinal-0 行为保持不变；initial、empty-direct successor、receipt-bearing content-free successor、content-bearing successor 与 ordinal-31 五个 compiled cross-language shape，以及 stale/non-ready/decision-source drift、predecessor/content closure、wrong-pin/handshake/bounds、atomic current-state revalidation 与 metadata privacy/effect sentinels均已覆盖。该 deterministic artifact 可在 B 后立即 stale，不是 consent、consumed grant、lifecycle、lane claim、provider send、current execution authority 或 Core sandbox/attestation；后续 effectful step 仍必须在 `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` 内完成 snapshot-to-claim CAS、fresh exact-request/conditional content consent 与单赢家 no-resend claim。
- [x] **Effectful scheduled one-node step**:一次调用至多消费一个 Core-selected `ready` node，并继续复用 exact request release、fresh per-request consent、global Project lane、one-shot send 与 post-claim no-resend quarantine。**stop_condition:** storage observation、successor-capable Core release parity、fresh consent binding、snapshot-to-claim CAS、重复调用/claim 竞争、exact-owner sidecar safety、hard-crash/uncertain commit、独立安全复审与正式 clean-clone acceptance 已全部闭合；仍不得自动恢复、重试或执行第二个节点。
- [x] **Durable whole-Graph controller**:SQLite v29 为 schedule-v1 serial Graph 增加 bounded append-only controller header/event journal；公开 `controller start/show/advance/step` 显式重入并可在一个 completed node 后被动准备 content-free successor，但每个外部请求仍需自身 exact consent，单次 `step` 至多 poll 一次。裸 `DispatchPlanned` 缺少或暂不可读 lifecycle 时永久停止为 `claimed_unknown`；只有 durable `RetryablePreclaimFailure` 可重新进入 fresh consent。claimed/quarantined/adjudicated/failed/failed-uncertain、兼容性与预算终态均不自动恢复、重试、重发或退款；schedule-v1 不开放 concurrent wave execution。Sprint 140 已完成独立治理/安全复核与正式 clean-clone acceptance；ADR-0099 仍为 Proposed。
- [x] **Strict Hub schema ownership validation(ADR 0012 及后续协议延伸)**:每个 canonical v0–v29 声明版本及 historical endpoint-only v25 migration source 在任何迁移 DDL 前先按不可变 DDL 复验（v0 必须空 catalog），迁移提交前再复验完整 v29 Hub catalog、columns/keys/FK、structural/index exact contract 与独占 main catalog。v27 从 exact journal 原子 backfill semantic projection，v28 增加 immutable Project Run direct-parent lineage，v29 增加 bounded scheduled whole-Graph controller header/event journal；旧 CHECK/PK/UQ/FK/index 漂移、额外 table/view/trigger/virtual shadow 或 backfill/final validation 失败均整链回滚且不自动修复。
- [x] **Durable Project Run + Responses adapter(ADR 0008)**:SQLite v1→v2 追加执行配置、增量语义 cursor、同快照 inspection、`runs`/`run_events`/Run-assistant 关联，persistence-first sink 保证 `tool_started` 在工具效果前提交；`run start/list/show`、bounded causal history、terminal/incomplete/pending-tool 判定、原子 assistant 写回与 completed-terminal crash-repair 均已交付。默认 deterministic/offline；显式 `--live` 默认零工具，仅 exact `--allow-read` 授权，并启用固定 HTTPS origin、无 redirect/隐式 retry、`store:false` 完整 output-item 回放、phase-aware final projection、terminal identity/status 校验及完整传输/token 上限；incomplete 永不释放工具。自动 execution resume、写/进程工具、remote 与 Web 不在本项。
- [x] **Explicit durable Project Run resume（bounded recovery slice）**：新增 `run resume RUN_ID`，只从已验证 durable journal 的安全 continuation point 继续；重放既有 history/message，不重复已提交的 `tool_started` effect，`tool_finished` 后缺失的 Tool message 可补写并从下一 turn 继续，已提交 answer 可补 terminal。未决 `tool_started` 外部效果、无 journal 或 Project 绑定不一致时 fail closed；仅 `RunOutcome::Completed` 的已完成终态 Run 会在 Project 绑定后、credential/provider/tool/history setup 前进入幂等 assistant Conversation writeback reconcile，且不读 workspace 内容，`RunOutcome::Failed`、`RunOutcome::Cancelled` 与 `RunOutcome::LimitExceeded` 终态仍拒绝 resume。该恢复协议不自动创建分支，也不提供 remote 或 whole-Graph resume，且不会自行扩大某个 Run 已持久化的工具面；ADR-0108 的第一方 Agent candidate 可按已持久化 mode/toolset 恢复其只读或 `--dev` edit/process 工具。provider usage 不从历史事件伪造重建。
- [x] **Bounded Project Run explain query**：新增只读 `run explain RUN_ID`，从已验证 journal 输出 event-level evidence、消息 role/长度/hash、工具 outcome、显式 workspace read scope、上下文缺口与安全 continuation 判定。未快照的 Conversation history、外部 effect truth 和 authenticated Grant/Approval/PDP 不会被伪造为 explanation fact；terminal/pending/incomplete 分支均有回归覆盖。
- [x] **Prepared Project Run restart（bounded independent rerun slice）**：新增 `run restart SOURCE_RUN_ID`，只接受已验证终态 source，以 source 指纹与显式 idempotency key 确定性创建同 Project/Conversation/user Prompt/execution configuration 的新 Run，并仅提交一个可恢复 seed；换 source 复用 key 会冲突，晚到精确重试返回目标 Run 的真实状态。准备阶段只校验 Project 路径，不读 credential/workspace 内容、不触发 provider/tool/network，不复制 source journal/result/answer；实际执行仍需显式 `run resume NEW_RUN_ID`，restart 本身保持 independent rerun 语义。
- [x] **Queryable Project Run root-input branch（bounded lineage slice）**：新增 `--idempotency-key KEY -C PATH run branch PARENT_RUN_ID`，只从已验证 terminal parent 在一个 immediate transaction 中原子创建 child Run、immutable direct-parent lineage 与单个 fresh `run_started`。child 复用 Project/Conversation/user Prompt/exact execution configuration，不复制 parent suffix/result/answer/tool events，并仅在显式 `run resume CHILD_RUN_ID` 后执行。`run lineage RUN_ID` 是 content-free 只读 direct-parent query；lineage 固定 `root_input`/seq 1，context/workspace snapshot 均未绑定，任意 event-prefix branching 仍需独立协议。
- [ ] **First-party Dev Agent v1（ADR-0108 Proposed implementation candidate；待终审与正式验收）**：`forge-runtime -C PATH agent [--dev] PROMPT|-` 复用唯一 Rust AgentRuntime、Responses adapter、Conversation/Run Hub 与显式 resume；默认版本化只读工具面，Unix `--dev` 才开放 workspace edit 与 same-user process。Prompt、Run、`run_started` 与 matching user message 在一个 SQLite immediate transaction 中形成完整 seed；只有 `Created` pristine seed 可自动发起 provider，请求精确重放只返回既有 terminal outcome（completed 时幂等 reconcile assistant）或要求 incomplete 显式 resume。所有 execution owner 通过同一 Hub cooperative OS lock 保守串行，在 provider/tool 前拒绝并发竞争；该锁不提供 same-user security 或 remote exactly-once。所选 workspace 的 canonical path、filesystem identity、Runtime factory 与全部工具共享一次 descriptor-anchored 打开。该 candidate 不是 OS/network sandbox、production approval、remote deploy、multi-Agent 或自动 Roadmap/Graph driver；只有同一冻结树通过 fresh review、完整 gates 与正式 `forge accept` 后才可勾选。
  本项公开容量仅为 per-Run 与 history projection 边界，不限制 Hub 生命周期总增长或完整 Conversation/Hub 查询的 SQL/内存工作；v1 不提供 retention、prune 或总存储 quota，仍由 operator 管理。
- [x] **声明式生产交付边界(ADR 0005)**:Deploy/Rollback 使用精确 emit 权限、固定最小 prompt、operator-pinned Claude executable bytes(非供应商身份)、整树 postflight、严格 JSON/verdict、validation receipt 与 source/artifact freshness；人审 marker 才能推进，远程执行保持外置。
- [x] **Durable lifecycle-driven migration(ADR 0016)**:`forge migrate --to-lifecycle production [--apply]` 只把显式持久晋升事件映射到 production；Explorer 同事务迁为 Engineering 并稳定注入五项补债，其他已知 mode 只改 lifecycle，已 production 不追溯。manual/lifecycle 共用 run lock、canonical intent WAL、before-image CAS、独立 receipt 与确定性 roll-forward；dry/transient flags 零写入，run/evolve 双检 terminal receipt 形成不可静默降级的持久治理 floor，status 暴露 pending/operation/recovery。
**明确遗留缺口(诚实标注,不夸大):**
- Agent 阶段默认 dry-run(`DryRunExecutor` 只叙述,安全默认);真实执行器需显式 `--executor command`。Claude prompt 走 stdin，子进程环境最小化；额外变量须 `--agent-env` 精确授权。未经本轮用户授权不重复烧付费模型预算。
- 原生 Go YAML 子集解析器为主，`harness/yaml2json.py` 仅作兼容回退；未知格式/契约版本均失败关闭。
- 独立 `agent-os` 仓库仍按 ADR 0003 等待远程位置与批准；Web UI/完整多厂商 Router 属 v3。Firecracker runner 已接入并本机 KVM 实测(见 docs/external-resource-verification.md)；
跨厂商 LiteLLM 验证已用本地 Ollama 完成(第二厂商付费凭证仍缺)。
- 远程部署/回滚是明确非目标：ForgeOS 只生成和验证声明式交付包，外部 CI/operator 实施并由人写结构化 approval marker；command-mode release 当前因开放 FD pin 契约只支持 Linux，其他平台失败关闭。

## v3 — AI 软件工厂
完整 coding-workspace Sandbox 契约 + 生产跨厂商池(LiteLLM)+ 预算治理 + 完整 Discover + Web UI。Docker/Firecracker runner 与 LiteLLM 主机验证已前置交付；动态迁移已由 ADR 0016 前置交付。
- [x] **R0-A Local App Server bootstrap v1（ADR-0100 Proposed）**：新增 Go `forge-server`，只接受显式 canonical absolute identity-bound dedicated state directory 与 literal loopback listener，以 euid-owned non-writable state-parent bound root、symlink/untrusted-owner/nonsticky-writable ancestor rejection、single-link lock identity 和 `GOOS=linux`（排除 Android）nonblocking advisory lock 保证一个 accepted physical namespace 内的 cooperating 单实例；Linux non-initial user namespace 中所有折叠到 configured overflow UID 且不在 inside map 的 host principals 均属 supervisor TCB。Android 与其他所有目标因 v1 没有 descriptor-bound 独立 ACL 模型校验而在 state access 前失败关闭。startup receipt 成功后才接受 HTTP，公开面仅为 exact-authority `GET|HEAD /api/v1/health`，并有连接/请求上限。本切片无 `control.db`、Runtime/Hub/Harness bridge、ambient workspace discovery、project/provider/credential 访问、出站网络、CORS、product command/query、Objective/Timeline/Approval/Outcome 或 Web UI，不把 process health 冒充依赖或产品就绪。
- [x] **R0-B1 Platform Core Identity + Envelope v1（ADR-0101 Proposed）**：冻结 typed opaque ID、contiguous Scope/Actor/Record reference、`ArtifactRef`、`CommandEnvelope`、`EventEnvelope`、独立 envelope/payload schema version 和 bounded canonical JSON；Go/Rust/Python 绑定共享 exact golden。它只验证 supplied bytes 与关系，不生成 live ID，不接 App Server/SQLite/Runtime transport/CAS，不签发 authority、append event、推进状态或证明 Artifact 存在。Execution/Verification Receipt、状态机、journal 与真实 Objective→Outcome consumer 仍属后续切片。
- [x] **R0-B2 Platform Core Receipt + State v1（ADR-0102 Proposed）**：冻结纯 `ExecutionReceipt`、`VerificationRequest/Receipt`、WorkItem/Attempt/Action 状态边、七类 broad rejection code、三语言 exact golden 与共享 mutation corpus。它只验证 caller-supplied observation/reference/edge，不生成或持久化 Receipt，不读取 Artifact bytes，不解析或认证 Grant/Approval/Evidence 等被引用记录，不做 reference resolution，不执行 Harness check，不读取或推进 current state，也不做完成裁决；Runtime replay、journal/current-version authority、兼容矩阵、legacy mapping 与真实 Objective→Outcome consumer 继续开放。
- [x] **R0-C1 Control Store Journal Foundation v1（ADR-0103 Proposed）**：Go `forge-server` 在 descriptor-bound instance lock 后、listener/readiness 前打开 exact private SQLite `control.db` v1；非空库先只读预检，canonical Command/Event、Go-owned aggregate expected-version/current head、component sequence、store-assigned global replay order、durable causation index、idempotency result 与 outbox 在一个 immediate transaction 中提交，外部 canonical Event inbox 按 explicit source stream 识别 replay/conflict/gap/reorder。schema/catalog/application identity、WAL/FULL/foreign-key/defensive profile、known sidecars、digest/canonical replay 与 relational consistency 均失败关闭。本切片仍无产品 Command/Query API、Space/Objective/Change repository、projection worker、live Runtime/Harness transport、Receipt producer、completion controller 或 UI；通过只能关闭 FC-03 的基础子集。
- [x] **R0-C2 Workspace Catalog Application Service v1（ADR-0104 Proposed）**：在 schema v1 不变的前提下，以 isolated Go `workspace/domain→application←store` 边界交付 immutable Space/Project/ProjectSnapshot-reference create/get/list；所有写入派生 exact Platform Core Command/Event 并复用 expected-version、idempotency 与 component sequence，所有读取从 canonical journal bounded replay。Project path 仅为从不打开的 `declared_unverified` lexical declaration，Snapshot observation 仅为 `declared_unresolved` RecordRef；无 alias/path uniqueness、filesystem capture、authenticated actor、public API、Objective/Change、Runtime/Harness worker、projection 或 UI。本项只关闭 FC-04。
- [x] **R0-C3 Delivery Domain v1（ADR-0105 Proposed）**：新增 Go 内部 pure `internal/delivery/domain`，冻结 Objective、Change desired/observed state、AcceptanceCriteria、snapshot-bound WorkGraph/WorkItem、预算与 DAG 校验及 caller-supplied snapshot drift comparison；同时 scoped harden 用于 no-production-consumer 证明的共享内部 `execbound` 与 machine parsers（bounded stdin、单管道普通 combined capture、count/drain degradation fail-closed），但不把它们变成 Delivery consumer 或 containment。它不新增跨语言 wire、Command/Event、SQLite schema/repository、identity/time source、Reconciler、认证/授权、Runtime/Harness transport、completion join、API 或 UI；本项只关闭 FC-05 的 pure domain 子集。
- [x] **R0-C4 Pure Pre-Effect Reconciler v1（ADR-0106 Proposed）**：新增 Go `internal/reconcile/application` 作为 Delivery Domain 的 exact sole production consumer；对 caller-supplied Objective/Change/WorkGraph、current snapshot IDs 与 version-bound Policy/Approval/Budget declared assessment 重新校验，并以固定优先级返回一个 `NoOp|AwaitApproval|ReadyWorkItem|BlockWorkItem|ReplanChange|EscalateUncertain`。`ReadyWorkItem` 只是零效果候选，不请求状态边、不创建或 dispatch Attempt、不认证 authority、不消费 Receipt、不完成 WorkItem/Change，也无 journal/outbox/controller/API/UI；本项只关闭 FC-06 pure pre-effect selection 子集。
- [x] **R0-C5 Runtime Attempt Request Domain v1（ADR-0107 Proposed / FR-03a）**：在 Rust domain crate 中把 caller-supplied `AttemptRequestInput` 验证并防御性复制为 private-field immutable `AttemptRequest`，绑定完整 Scope、explicit Attempt/WorkItem/Project/ProjectSnapshot refs、四项 Control aggregate versions、executor declaration、可选 context Artifact/workspace capability/Grant、normalized Approval/effect sets、budget、timeout、idempotency，并把初始 state 固定为 `requested`。本项已实现并验收；它无 lifecycle reducer、Session/Turn/Action、SQLite/journal/outbox、wire/protocol、Artifact resolution、authenticated authority/budget reservation、Reconciler consumer、dispatch 或任何 effect，只关闭 FR-03a pure request 子集，不关闭完整 FR-03 或 FC-07。
- [x] **R0-C6 Runtime Attempt Lifecycle Domain v1（ADR-0109 Proposed / FR-03b；条件化 DONE，遵守 Sprint 150 completion_boundary）**：在 `execution::attempt` 的 ADR-0107 frozen boundary 外增加 sibling pure Rust `AttemptLifecycle`，只允许从 `requested` seed 经 closed seven-variant `AttemptTransitionRequest` 调用既有 Platform Core validator，返回新的 private-state value。它不复制状态图，不暴露 raw/Unknown target、generic Uncertain、reset/retry/reopen，不创建 Attempt identity/version/aggregate，不消费 `AttemptRequest`，也无 Session/Turn/Action、serde/wire、SQLite/journal/outbox、budget/usage、authority、effect 或产品入口；完成也只关闭 FR-03b，完整 FR-03、FR-04、FR-06 与 FC-07 保持开放。

- [x] **R0-C7 Runtime Attempt Admission Journal v1（ADR-0110 Proposed / FR-04a；条件化 DONE，遵守 Sprint 151 completion_boundary）**：在独立 Rust infrastructure SQLite profile 中，将 immutable request、固定 `requested` creation Event 与 retained pending outbox 原子提交；支持精确幂等重放、完整有界重开校验与分页。只接收 caller 显式打开并移交的 on-disk Connection，不提供安全文件 opener、不迁移旧 Hub、不推进 lifecycle、不 ack/send、不产生执行权限；FR-04 后续观察证据与状态推进、FR-06/FC-07 和产品入口仍开放，完成须通过 Sprint 151 同树验收。

### 个人多端会话与设备执行（已批准实施；实现进行中，尚未交付）

用户已批准将个人单账号、多端共享会话和多设备资源纳入近期实施路线；本批准更新产品优先级，不改变任何历史完成状态，也不替代 ADR 的正式治理生命周期。Sprint 152–155 建立 Rust Hub change journal、Go→Rust bridge、Prompt-history 与 bounded Conversation bootstrap；Sprint 156 接入 Snaplink JWT/scopes、Hub 精确 owner、认证会话/Prompt API、私网 TLS、浏览器 Origin allowlist 和 owner-local dense replay feed。CLI/TUI 现提供 remote login、会话/Prompt 命令、remote changes list 与 sync；使用保存登录时，二者共用按 Coordinator 与 exact owner 分区的本地断线游标，游标文件沿用 Unix 私有目录/文件保护。CLI/TUI 仍以 Unix 权限保护文件保存 access token；Linux/macOS 另以 Secret Service/Keychain 保存 refresh token 并自动续期，其他 CLI 平台仍不支持安全持久 refresh。显式 `FORGE_ACCESS_TOKEN` 覆盖模式不读写持久游标。Flutter Console /forge/ 使用 client-scoped 登录 token，在 Web 的 localStorage 与原生平台偏好中持久化 owner-local cursor；本地键绑定 API origin、Forge client/resource 与 issuer/tenant/subject 的 SHA-256 摘要，不保存 token，且只有变更页及其所需会话/历史刷新成功后才推进。opaque 或不合格 token 只使用本次进程内游标。默认客户端仍使用轮询；CLI/TUI 与 Console 已提供显式 opt-in change-stream 读取。现有 `/prompts` 仍只持久化 Prompt，不启动 Run；Hub v34 另提供原子 owner-scoped pending Run-intent 与私有 Go Runtime bridge，但没有 public intent/consent route；Go 内部已有 owner-filtered Project identity lookup 与 fail-closed profile catalog，且已接入 `forge-server` 启动绑定；尚未接入任何 HTTP route。Run intent 的回执不是执行状态。这些仍是 partial slice，尚未经过 formal acceptance。完整 Web/App/Mobile 跨端旅程、权威设备库存/调度、Runner 和远程执行仍未交付，snapshot_at_cursor 全量加载仍不可远程使用。P1–P4 继续按各自退出门验证。

- **P1 — 个人会话 Coordinator**：已实现 Snaplink claims/scopes 验证、Hub owner-enforced 的 Global/Project/Group 会话创建与列表、历史分页、幂等 Prompt 写入与 dense owner-local paged replay feed。Hub v34 另有不创建 Run 的原子 pending Run-intent 和 owner-scoped timeline；它只可经内部 Runtime bridge 调用。App Server 必须 pin 单一 Coordinator `(issuer, subject, tenant_id)`，避免同租户其他账号把会话关联到本地 Project/Group；Conversation scope 仅作组织信息，Project scope 本身不授予项目执行权限。CLI/TUI 使用保存登录时，已按 Coordinator 与 exact owner 保存并恢复本机 replay cursor；Flutter Console 在 Web/native 平台本地保存 hash-bound cursor，并只在对应历史刷新成功后推进。Forge Console 退出现会依次请求撤销本端 access/refresh token，再只清本地 Forge credential slot；Snaplink `/token/revoke` 已支持 public `none` client authentication，但只撤销绑定该 exact `client_id` 的 token，提交有效的 active refresh token 时再对其 rotation family 做受限级联，过期或已消费 token 仍是 oracle-safe 200 no-op；Forge App Server 可 opt-in 逐请求 confidential introspection，保留 issuer/audience/tenant/subject 固定。后端失败时 Console 仍完成本机退出，未实现离线重试队列。公开 Run-intent 接口仍等待 profile catalog 的 HTTP 接入、显式 Project consent UX 和 formal acceptance；live delivery 和正式接受仍未完成。部署仍需 provision 独立 confidential Forge resource-server client/secret，并验证所有端的 issuer/subject/tenant 投影及 Forge audience。Conversation/Prompt/Run 的 canonical owner 仍为 Rust Hub，Go 不复制写入 Hub canonical state。退出门：跨进程鉴权、授权、重放、撤销、重试语义通过安全和兼容性验证。
- **P2 — 多端客户端**：目标仍是 CLI、TUI、Web、桌面 App、Mobile 共用版本化 API/事件合同，能查看同一账号不同实例创建的会话并提交 Prompt；登录会话、Forge Conversation 与 Aero IM room 分离。CLI/TUI 已能选择 Global/Project/Group 创建 scope，CLI 默认筛选当前页，也可用 `remote sessions list --all` 扫描最多 64 页并跨页筛选 scope；TUI `filter global|project:ID|group:ID` / `filter clear` 仅筛选本地已加载列表，空匹配页仍按服务端原 cursor 翻页，筛选不代表授权或设备身份。真实共享会话 E2E 经 Go HTTP→Rust Hub 写入 130 个 owner-bound 会话，再以第二个 CLI 身份跨两页读取并验证顺序、完整可见性和 foreign subject 空结果，普通与 race-enabled E2E 均通过。TUI 显示 scope，并可从本机 owner-bound 游标恢复变更读取；CLI `remote prompts list` 与 TUI `older` 现可用 Hub 游标翻阅较早 Prompt 页，客户端会验证页记录严格早于请求游标；Flutter Console `/forge/` 已有 owner-hash-bound 持久游标和只读 Run 摘要/时间线，TUI 也能查看所选会话的 Run 列表及 metadata-only 事件时间线。新增真实 Go HTTP→Rust Hub 集成会在同一临时 Hub/Coordinator 中启动独立 `forge-runtime` CLI 子进程：A 创建，B 列表并追加 Prompt，A 读取历史/变更；foreign C 通过认证后只得到空列表与 not-found，且 Hub 中无 Run/pending intent、HTTP allowlist 中无设备或调度调用。新增的可选 Flutter live E2E 使用 Console 的 ForgeConversationsApi 服务，并将同一个签名测试 token 写入 Forge 专用 client slot、通过 ForgeSessionsGate 和编译时 Coordinator origin 加载实际 ForgeSessionsScreen：页面读取其他客户端创建的会话与 Prompt，并通过界面追加；Go 再读回 Hub 结果。HTTP allowlist 仅允许会话列表、历史、追加、变更流和只读 Run 页。真实 TUI E2E 也可在 util-linux PTY 中启动 `forge-runtime remote tui`，读取同一会话并追加 Prompt；录制请求严格限制为会话列表、历史读取、Prompt 追加与写后历史刷新，Go 再从 Hub 回读。上述测试验证 Flutter gate/widget/API-service 与真实 TUI 进程经过 Go→Rust 的共享会话路径，不验证浏览器部署、实际桌面/Mobile 设备体验或其 OAuth 生命周期，仍不完成跨所有端验收。RFC 8628 登录、Unix access-token 文件存储、Linux/macOS CLI/TUI refresh（refresh token 使用 Secret Service/Keychain，并以本机锁串行轮换）和 Console refresh 已落地，但 P2 仍未完成；Windows/Android 等 CLI 平台仍无受支持的安全持久化后端，pending Run-intent/consent 仍无公开客户端流程，完整 Web/App/Mobile 生命周期和 live delivery 尚未验收。新增的 `forge-shared-session-v1.json` 将 Conversation list/detail、Prompt history、owner-local change feed 和 Prompt append receipt 固定为一份跨 Go/Rust/Flutter 的 fixture；Go、Rust CLI/TUI 与 Flutter 均提供 owner-bound detail 读取，foreign/missing ID 共享 not-found 语义，契约脚本已验证三端 exact-field、identity、cursor/order 与 append-role parity。Web gate 另验证 Forge tab-scoped credentials 不覆盖 Admin slot。退出门仍要求跨客户端一致性、断线游标恢复、重复/乱序处理及客户端契约测试通过。
  P2 原生前后台行为补充：Flutter 生命周期测试覆盖应用暂停时停止 15 秒轮询、恢复时立即同步 owner 变更流并刷新会话；这是 widget-binding 回归，不等同于真实 Android/iOS 设备及系统 OAuth 验收。
  P2 浏览器补充证据：可选的真实 Chromium E2E 将 `/forge/` Flutter Web bundle 置于临时同源 Coordinator 下，使用 Forge 专用 tab `sessionStorage` 测试 token 打开真实页面、读取共享会话并从界面追加 Prompt；HTTP allowlist 限制为会话/历史读取、Prompt 追加、变更流和只读 Run 查询，Go 再从 Hub 回读。它覆盖测试托管下的浏览器 runtime，不验证生产代理/部署、跨源 CORS 或真实 OAuth；测试需显式设置 `FORGE_BROWSER_E2E=1` 并提供 Python Playwright 与 Chromium。
 P2 原生安全凭据补充：Flutter Forge 在 Android/iOS/Linux/macOS/Windows 使用 OS secure storage，支持冷启动恢复、refresh rotation 与 Forge-only 清理；Web 保持 tab `sessionStorage`。Linux 需要 libsecret 和可用的 D-Bus Secret Service，Windows 构建需要 ATL。Rust CLI/TUI 的持久 refresh 支持 Linux/macOS OS keyring；其他 CLI 平台暂不支持安全持久登录。Flutter 凭据和刷新聚焦测试及 analyze 通过；本轮桌面原生构建/真实 OS keyring 未验证。详情见实现计划 §44、§46。
97.  P2 断线与桌面并发补充：Flutter Forge 保存 owner-bound 的 Conversation 元数据快照（不含 Prompt、Run、token 或 owner claim），网络失败时以 stale/offline 状态展示，恢复后自动替换并在 Forge sign-out 清除；Linux/macOS/Windows 的刷新轮换使用按客户端 `FileLock` 并在锁内重载安全凭据，避免独立桌面进程重复消费 single-use refresh token；Forge GET 读取对传输失败及 408/425/429/5xx 做有界三次重试，POST 写入仍由显式幂等重试恢复。全量 Flutter 测试、analyze、Web 与 Android 构建通过；Linux 仍受 libsecret 开发依赖限制，真实 macOS/Windows 多进程验证待补。详情见实现计划 §§49–51。
- **P2 Run observer 补充**：`forge-run-observer-resume-v1` 固定 metadata-only Run timeline 的 `after_sequence → scanned_through_sequence` 连续恢复和无跳号事件，Go、Rust CLI 与 Flutter 通过同一 fixture 校验 Conversation/Run 绑定、边界与游标推进。它不提供 Run 写入、执行恢复、live delivery、设备库存、调度或 Runner 派发；完整 durable Run 内容、实时交付和跨端正式验收仍开放。详情见实现计划 §63。
- **P3a — 离线声明比较**：Go `forge device-placement dry-run` 已接受有界 caller-supplied JSON，输出确定性 exclusion reason，并明确所有字段未经验证、无选择目标且 execution/reservation/dispatch 均为 false。Go/Rust 共享测试向量覆盖资源/策略、审批/cordon/liveness、固定时间的 freshness 与 lease expiry，以及 GPU 缺失/声明显存不足/满足要求；新增 `forge-device-inventory-observation-v1` fixture 由 Go、Rust 和 Flutter 严格只读消费，固定 owner/instance/resource 字段、unverified 标记及 false authority bits。Reason 名称只在测试适配器中归一化，仍不是运行时 wire contract。它不发现、不连接、不持久化、不注册设备，也不代表当前在线/新鲜库存。P3a 的继续工作只限离线合同、验证和只读比较。
- **P3b — 实时库存（授权门控）**：目标包含显式 Runner 身份、独立凭证、带 TTL 的服务端 heartbeat 与 owner-scoped read-only inventory。ADR-0039 目前禁止 live device registration/heartbeat；ADR-0114 是 Proposed-only follow-up candidate，未授权真实注册或 heartbeat。只有适用 architecture/security decision 正式 Accepted 后才能实现或暴露；这期间不添加设备网络 API、库存持久化、reservation 或 dispatch。当前仅新增 Go/Rust 纯函数 identity proof/approval binding、heartbeat sequencing、heartbeat compare-and-swap plan、Go/Rust/Flutter inventory status projection consumers 与 owner-scoped snapshot canonicalization，固定 exact owner/key/challenge、pending-versus-approved、generation/sequence/TTL/服务器时间、revision conflict、freshness/status、stable row ordering/digest 拒绝语义，所有 identity/persistence/authority bits 保持 false。获批后的退出门仍需至少两台设备及过期、撤销、容量和不兼容反例；Flutter status/snapshot consumers 详情见实现计划 §§68–69。
- **P4 — 两设备受控执行（单独授权门）**：仅在 P3b 获正式批准并完成、隔离下限通过且另有 Accepted 执行/安全决策后，才增加显式 Placement、原子资源 reservation、lease/fencing、watchdog、取消/超时、Artifact 校验与审计 outbox。当前已冻结 Flutter 离线 placement parity consumer（实现计划 §71）和 Rust domain 的纯内存 lease/fencing、direct-argv Runner command/terminal receipt ABI 预研合同（实现计划 §70）；这些切片不提供时钟、持久化、网络、Runner、reservation、scheduler 或 dispatch 权威，不改变此门槛。退出门：两个已授权 Runner 可执行且仅一份有效租约能提交结果，过期/分区 Runner 无法提交，未知 effect 不静默重试。
- **P5 — 团队与规模化**：多用户/team ACL、多租户、HA/灾备、企业身份、配额与多 Coordinator federation 另行建 ADR 并置于个人单账号阶段之后。

### AI Engineering OS 治理知识层（ADR 0037–0063；蓝图 + shadow/contract-only 契约、local journal/semantic runtime、窄 authority profiles 及 AFDS 扩展）
- **DONE — Wave 0A 蓝图冻结**:能力中心化的「生命周期节点 × Skill × 显式权限」模型、00–16 节点 SOP、AADM 决策 ABI、Meta Reflection、default-off Device Fabric、Evidence/Claim/KG/Impact/Debt/Health/Grant/Review 契约、条件化 OOP/DI/AOP/DDD/事件驱动规范和规划期机读目录已归档于 `docs/design/ai-engineering-os/`；其中目标态与未勾选路线仍为 `planning_only`，ADR 0040–0058 的已交付合同/profile 按各自边界执行且不冒充完整 runtime。
- **DONE — Wave 0B Agent Engineering shadow 契约**:ADR 0040 在 `.agent/engineering` 固化 activation、14 学科、11 条分级规则、`forge accept` detector registry、typed Context 路由和 W0–W3 保障下限；TaskEvidencePackage 禁止自写完成裁决并要求 source-bound 结构化观察。严格 validator 拒绝重复 YAML key、anchor/alias、悬挂/逃逸路径、虚假 automatic detector、Context 语义降级与 profile 整体降级，并作为 `forge check` 第 13 项随 scaffold/legacy upgrade 继承。它只执行合同/接线完整性，不改变 runtime 路由或完成权威。
- **DONE — Wave 0C Backend decision shadow 契约**:ADR 0041 固化 16 类 L1–L4/W1–W3 后端触发下限、14 维决策序列、条件化 DTO/Domain/Persistence/Read 模型角色、持久化与不可逆决策关卡、十张密集 Skill adapter 和无自批权 BackendDecisionPackage。安全/数据/后端 Context route、独立 validator、50 个 package 对抗用例及 init/legacy-upgrade 回归已接入；policy/Schema 与逐项仓库证据绑定真实字节，proof type/class/subject、readiness dependency、ADR 与 Reviewer 关系被校验。完整 tree/context provenance 和受信 producer/reviewer attestation 仍待 runtime；当前 detector 明示 `shadow/load_bearing:false`，不冒充 pre-code runtime gate。
- **DONE — Wave 0D Frontend design shadow 契约 + Business UI Geometry 扩展**:ADR 0042 固化 20 类前端风险触发下限、14 维 AFDS 决策、12 个产品 Profile、14 个页面 Pattern、flow/state/action/permission 结构与引用合同及多源证据边界；ADR 0044 在不改变 FrontendDesignPackage v1 顶层 ABI、不新增 capability owner 的前提下，增加 supporting `ui-geometry` Skill、digest-bound `business_ui_composition` 与可选 `geometry_measurement_receipts`。三张 canonical Skill 仍分别拥有信息交互、Design System/无障碍和客户端实现；composition/report validator 只证明有界 JSON、引用、摘要与声明一致性，不证明真实浏览器/原生 Runner、视觉平衡或可信 producer。user-experience Context route、shadow detector、对抗 validator 及 init/legacy-upgrade 回归沿用同一治理主干；自动影响识别、pre-code authority 和可信执行/审查来源仍待 runtime。
- **DONE — Wave 0E Frontend code architecture shadow 契约**:ADR 0043 增加独立 procedural Skill，但不创建新 capability owner；项目合同显式声明 target/module/layer/public-test entrypoint 和依赖矩阵。独立 detector 使用项目 TypeScript Compiler API 解析 alias、extensionless 与 index import，区分 pass/fail/inconclusive/not_applicable；Vue/Dart 适配器缺失时不伪报通过。方向/所有权/循环/配置完整性不可 baseline/waive，God/目录/API 预算只产出审查信号；当前仍非 `forge accept` 载重门禁。
- **DONE — Wave 0F-A Canonical Evidence/Claim Kernel**:ADR 0045 交付 strict EvidenceRecord/KnowledgeClaim v1 schema、record/aggregate/sequence identity、kind-separated canonical SHA-256、状态矩阵与 authority-free shadow admissibility；Go/Rust/Python 共享 exact golden，universal checker 随 scaffold/upgrade 继承。它不写 Hub、不认证 producer、不确认事实、不接受决策、不满足 hard gate，也不导入旧 Memory/ADR。
- **DONE — Wave 0F-B–1 Local GovernanceRecordJournal**:ADR 0046 与 journal v1 schema 交付 bounded atomic append、exact replay、immutable bytes、contiguous structural history、metadata-first inspection、SQLite v25 additive-empty migration 与 rebuildable structural head；引用闭包最多加载 1,024 records、16,777,216 candidate-plus-closure canonical bytes，且从 candidate 到任一 transitive premise 最多 256 条 `derived_from` edges，这些都只是防资源耗尽的 admissibility limits。Rust domain/application/store、CLI、migration/compatibility、重建/并发/对抗测试与 scaffold/upgrade 回归已接线，并经独立复审和 `forge accept` 验收；scaffold 仍不安装 Rust binary，缺兼容 `forge-runtime` 时 persistence 为 `not_executed`。Structural head 明确不是 truth、authority、freshness、conflict winner 或 current knowledge，receipt 只有 `stored|exact_replay`。
- **DONE — Wave 0F-C–1 Shadow CognitiveAtom Projection v1**:ADR 0047 只把 strict shadow KnowledgeClaim 中的 `fact|constraint|decision|inference|assumption|hypothesis|unknown` 七类确定性投影为 CognitiveAtom；`lesson|proposal` 可留在 source closure，但不会生成 Atom。投影固定为 `projection_mode:shadow`、`hardness:none`、`authority_ref:null`、`instruction_allowed:false`，只返回 `PROJECTED_SHADOW`；它是无副作用的 Claim→Atom pure projection，不是 prompt/model compiler、完整 Kernel ABI、truth/authority/transition/effect/completion attestation，也不写 journal 或其他 persistence。
- **DONE — Wave 0F-B–2 Local Governance Semantic View v1**:ADR 0054 在 exact current journal tail 上交付可重建 projection、authority-free lifecycle continuity、显式 caller-time validity/review/validation labels、deterministic conflict candidates 与 Assumption/Hypothesis validation schedules；caller time 不回选历史 tail。SQLite v27 对 append/replay/backfill/read/rebuild 从 exact records 重算并失败关闭，backfill/rebuild 完整重验 history/reference relations，live read-only CLI 在单一 Deferred snapshot 内受 record/byte/work budgets 约束且必须显式 `--as-of-unix-ms`；输出固定 `semantic_projection_only_no_truth_or_authority`，不选 winner、不执行 validation、不推进权威状态。
- **DONE — Wave 0F-B–3a Shadow ContextPackage v1**:ADR 0055 交付 strict/versioned/bounded 的 authority-free pure builder：caller 显式绑定 task/source/time/policy/routes/tree/tokenizer，required-first 选择与 optional omission receipts、typed trust lanes、declared byte-range redaction、UTF-8 truncation、exact projection token budget、六类 domain-separated digest 和 cache full revalidation 在 Python/Go/Rust 对同一 golden 逐字节一致。结果仅为 `ASSEMBLED_SHADOW`，不读 repository、不调用 provider、不持久化，也不签发 truth/instruction/permission/approval/completion/effect；真实 Context Router、prompt compiler、source authentication 与 Grant/PDP integration 仍未实现。
- **DONE — Wave 0F-B–3b-1 CapabilityGrant v1 contract-only**:ADR 0056 只冻结 strict/versioned/bounded `CapabilityGrant` envelope、21 个单-effect vocabulary、typed allow/deny scope、caller-time/budget/binding/SoD 关系及 authority-neutral declared assessment；Python/Go/Rust 对 exact golden 的 canonical bytes/digests/relations 一致。Registry 保持其为 `shipped_contract_only_kinds`，shadow detector 不载重；它不认证 issuer/proof/principal/policy/Approval，不评价 revocation/usage，不做 pre/postflight、receipt、persistence 或 effect，也不产生 authorization/permission attestation。
- **DONE — Wave 0F-B–3b-2 Authenticated bootstrap repo-read Grant issuance profile**:ADR 0057 仅交付 `authenticated_bootstrap_repo_read_grant_issuance_v1`：由 operator 部署的独立非 Agent `forge-kernel` 读取 repo 外显式 pinned trust root、signed policy 与 signed GrantRequest，只为 `bootstrap_planning` 的 `repository-reader/v1`、单一 `repo.read`、exact paths、小预算/TTL 以及 `local|development|test` 签发 signed `CapabilityGrant` 和 durable signed `GrantIssuanceReceipt`。Runtime 仅支持 Unix；authority/repository resolved endpoint 按 ancestor filesystem identity 双向不重叠且 repository source/resolution/opened identity 全 session 绑定；authority/state directory exact `0700`、叶子 exact `0600`/euid-owned/single-link/无特殊权限位，非 Unix 在读 authority 材料前失败关闭。这些本地文件检查不是 OS principal/HSM 隔离，同 UID 测试也不是 production trust proof。Scaffold/upgrade 只复制合同和结构 checker，不安装 binary/root/key/state，缺 runtime 必须为 `not_executed`；`forge accept` 仍只是完成权威，绝不是 issuer。

- **DONE — Pure session-to-device placement observation（cross-device plan §74）**：共享 `forge-session-placement-observation-v1` 将 caller-declared owner tuple、Conversation ID、Run ID 与固定时间 placement parity 结果绑定；Go `deviceplacement`、Rust Runtime domain、Flutter Console `forge_session_placement` 保留 `(device_id, instance_id)`、稳定 exclusion reasons 与 `matches_requirements`。duplicate instance、owner/session binding、candidate mismatch fail closed；selected target 始终为空，identity/heartbeat/inventory/reservation/execution/dispatch authority bits 始终 false。合同脚本覆盖三端和 malformed duplicate case；无 endpoint、clock、storage、registration、scheduler、reservation、Runner 或 process execution。ADR-0039 仍 planning-only，ADR-0114 仍 Proposed/null。
- **DONE — TUI offline inventory inspection（cross-device plan §75）**：Rust `remote tui` 新增 `inventory show --input FILE`，在交互式 TUI 中渲染与 CLI 相同的 bounded `forge-device-inventory-observation-v1` 声明；为保持 TUI 输入流完整，stdin 形式仍通过 standalone CLI 使用。输出保留资源字段及 unverified/authority-false 标记，聚焦测试确认不发 `/api/v1/devices` 请求。该路径不打开设备 API、不联网、不读时钟、不持久化、不注册、不选 target、不 reservation、不 scheduling、不 dispatch、不 Runner execution；ADR-0039 仍 planning-only，ADR-0114 仍 Proposed/null。
- **DONE — Pure Run-intent observation across session and placement（cross-device plan §76）**：共享 `forge-run-intent-observation-v1` 将 payload-free Prompt acceptance receipt、已观察 Run 摘要与既有 session placement observation 绑定；Go `deviceplacement`、Rust Runtime domain、Flutter Console 输出同一 preview metadata（Conversation/Prompt/Run 精确关联、Run 状态/序列、placement decision/eligible-instance 计数、replay）。`preview_only=true`，Prompt content 不进入合同，selected device/instance 始终为空，owner/device 声明保持 unverified，identity/heartbeat/inventory/reservation/execution/dispatch authority bits 始终 false。三端 strict fixture 与 confused-binding tests 已接入合同脚本；无 Prompt/Run 创建、clock、Hub/Runner、storage、registration、scheduler、reservation、dispatch 或 process execution。ADR-0039 仍 planning-only，ADR-0114 仍 Proposed/null。
- **DONE — CLI/TUI offline Run-intent preview（cross-device plan §77）**：Rust CLI 新增 `device placement run-intent-preview --input RUN_FILE|- --placement-input SESSION_FILE|-`，TUI 新增双文件本地查看入口。两者严格消费 Run-intent/session-placement fixtures，复算 domain placement 后调用纯 Run-intent observer，并稳定输出 payload-free receipt、Run 状态/序列及 placement/eligible 计数。unknown/oversized/confused binding fail closed，selected target 为空且全部 authority bits 为 false；聚焦测试确认 TUI 不发设备请求。无 Prompt/Run 创建、Hub/Runner、clock、storage、selection、reservation、dispatch 或 execution；ADR-0039 仍 planning-only，ADR-0114 仍 Proposed/null。

- **DONE — CLI/TUI offline inventory snapshot canonicalization (cross-device plan §81)**：Rust CLI 新增 `device inventory snapshot-canonical --input FILE|-`，严格消费 `forge-device-inventory-snapshot-canonical-v1`，复用 domain canonicalizer/digest，输出稳定 `(device_id, instance_id)` 顺序及 digest/error；`remote tui` 新增 `inventory snapshot-canonical --input FILE`，保持交互 stdin 可用。unknown/duplicate/oversized/authority/expected mismatch fail closed，focused tests确认无 `/api/v1/devices` 请求；digest 只是未验证声明的完整性标签，不是身份、freshness、reservation 或 execution authority；无 route、clock、storage、registration、heartbeat、selection、reservation、scheduling、dispatch、Runner 或 process execution，ADR-0039 仍 planning-only，ADR-0114 仍 Proposed/null，P4 仍需独立 Accepted execution/security decision。

- **DONE — Snaplink-authenticated inert execution intent over Rust Hub（cross-device plan §91B）**：新增默认跳过的 Go app-server E2E，启动真实 Snaplink SSO ephemeral TLS/JWKS，使用独立 `forge-console`/`forge-cli` token，通过仅测试可构造的 inert execution surface 接入真实 Rust Hub。token A 创建 Project Conversation 并 preview/grant consent，token B 提交并读取 pending Run intent/timeline；撤销后新 intent 返回 conflict，原幂等 key 仍 replay immutable receipt，普通 Runs 保持为空。生产 `newConversationRoutes` 对 consent/pending-intent 路径仍为 404；不增加 device enrollment/inventory/heartbeat、lease issuance、reservation/selection/scheduling/dispatch、Runner execution 或 audit outbox。ADR-0039 仍 planning-only，ADR-0114 仍 Proposed/null，P4 仍需独立 Accepted execution/security decision。
- **DONE — Snaplink introspection-backed authenticated session boundary (cross-device plan §91A)**: Added a test-only Forge app-server E2E over a real Snaplink ephemeral TLS handler. A public `forge-console` token creates/lists a Conversation and appends a Prompt through Forge's confidential `forge-introspector` `/token/introspect` path using a temporary 0600 secret file; a counting transport proves per-request introspection, and Snaplink revocation is observed immediately on the next request. Wrong audience/tenant, missing write scope, and wrong secret fail closed. The local `go.mod` replace points to the requested Snaplink ecosystem so signed `tenant_id` introspection provenance is present. No production execution/device/inventory/heartbeat/reservation/scheduling/dispatch/Runner/audit surface is enabled; ADR-0039 remains planning-only, ADR-0114/0113 remain Proposed/null, and P4 still needs separate Accepted execution/security governance.
- **DONE — Snaplink JWT consumed by the real CLI/TUI and Flutter shared-session clients (cross-device plan §92)**: Extended the §91B test-only E2E to invoke the actual `forge-runtime` binary with real Snaplink JWTs. Client A lists the owner Conversation, client B reads Prompt history, the ordinary Run page remains empty, and client B opens the same Conversation in the TUI and appends a Prompt. With `FORGE_CONSOLE_E2E=1`, Flutter Console API and widget tests use the same Snaplink JWT to cold-start, read the Conversation, and append idempotent Prompts; `FORGE_BROWSER_E2E=1` additionally serves the Flutter Web build and drives the browser path with that JWT. The test is conditional on `FORGE_RUNTIME_BIN`, uses temporary homes and ephemeral fixtures, and does not exercise host OS device-code credential persistence. No device/inventory/heartbeat authority, lease, reservation, scheduling, dispatch, Runner execution, or audit surface is enabled; ADR-0039 remains planning-only, ADR-0114/0113 remain Proposed/null, and P4 still requires separate Accepted execution/security governance.
- **DONE — Snaplink change-feed cursor consumed by the real CLI and TUI (cross-device plan §93)**: Extended the §92 authenticated process E2E so the real Rust CLI reads the owner-scoped change feed and verifies the dense Conversation-created and Prompt-appended cursors. The real Rust TUI runs `sync` with the same Snaplink token, advances through both events, refreshes the selected session and Prompt history, and appends a Prompt. Exact request allowlists cover only Conversation, Prompt, and change-feed reads plus the tested Prompt write. No Run, device/inventory/heartbeat authority, lease, reservation, scheduling, dispatch, Runner execution, or audit outbox is enabled; ADR-0039 remains planning-only, ADR-0114/0113 remain Proposed/null, and P4 still requires separate Accepted execution/security governance.
- **DONE — Real Snaplink RFC 8628 login and saved CLI credential consumption (cross-device plan §94)**: Added an opt-in Go app-server E2E that drives the actual Rust CLI through a temporary Snaplink device-code issuer, approves the displayed code with a Forge Console bearer, waits for token polling, checks owner-bound file and protected refresh-token storage, then starts a second CLI process that loads the saved credential and sends its Bearer token to the Forge session-list endpoint. The test rejects refresh-token material in the JSON file and verifies issuer/client/subject/tenant binding. It runs only with `FORGE_DEVICE_LOGIN_E2E=1` and an unlocked host Secret Service; no Forge device registration, heartbeat/inventory authority, Run, lease, reservation, scheduling, dispatch, Runner execution, or audit outbox is enabled; ADR-0039 remains planning-only, ADR-0114/0113 remain Proposed/null, and P4 still requires separate Accepted execution/security governance.
- **DONE — Saved CLI change-feed cursor resumed across processes (cross-device plan §95)**: Extended the opt-in RFC 8628 E2E so one real Rust CLI process consumes `remote changes list` from cursor 0 and persists its owner-bound checkpoint, then a second process with the same saved credential resumes at cursor 1 and receives an empty page. The temporary Forge endpoint asserts the exact cursor sequence and no duplicate replay. This remains read-replay continuity evidence only; no Forge device registration, heartbeat/inventory authority, Run, lease, reservation, scheduling, dispatch, Runner execution, or audit outbox is enabled. ADR-0039 remains planning-only, ADR-0113/0114 remain Proposed/null, and P4 still needs separate Accepted execution/security governance.
- **DONE — Authenticated offline placement preview boundary (cross-device plan §96)**: Configured Forge session routes now expose bounded `POST /api/v1/device-placement/preview` for the strict caller-supplied `forge.device-placement-dry-run/v1` declaration. - **DONE — Flutter authenticated placement preview consumer (cross-device plan §97)**: Flutter Console's Forge API client now serializes the strict bounded placement declaration and sends one authenticated `POST /api/v1/device-placement/preview`. Lifecycle pause, sign-out, authorization invalidation, disposal, and in-flight coalescing keep reads bounded; the owner-local cursor remains committed only after required authenticated GET refreshes succeed. Widget coverage proves the 15-to-30-second expansion and recovery. ## 280. Run observer preview and selected-Run metadata parity (2026-09-17)

Forge Runtime adds fixture-only local CLI/TUI previews for the content-free
`forge.run.observed.v1` value. Bounded strict decoding rejects duplicate or
unknown fields, content, unsafe metadata, and enabled authority bits before
metadata-only rendering. Snaplink Console's shared Web/App/Mobile Gate and
Sessions screen accept an explicit `ForgeRunObserved`, strictly re-decode it,
require the selected Conversation/Run binding, and render the opaque owner
digest with bounded Run metadata in a read-only card. The default Gate remains
request-free. No device route, inventory authority, target selection,
reservation, scheduling, dispatch, Runner, execution, receipt persistence, or
Audit behavior was added; ADR-0039 remains planning-only and ADR-0113/0114
remain Proposed/null.

### Cross-device plan §281 — Host-side native cold-start shared-session lifecycle

Snaplink Console now has a host-side `forge_mobile_shared_session_e2e_test.dart`
that uses the native `ForgeCredentialStore` abstraction with an injected secure
store, clears in-memory credentials across two cold starts, reads the same
owner Conversation, appends and replays one idempotent Prompt, and verifies
aggregate version, owner cursor, and one history row. Its initial
`android-host` marker kept the evidence honest about the absence of physical
Android instrumentation; §348 adds the matching `ios-host` variant. The opt-in
Go multiclient path launches the test through the
real JWT → Go → Rust path, asserts prompt/session/change-feed requests only,
rejects devices, Run-intents, placement, and dispatch, and rechecks detail and
history from Go. ADR-0039 remains planning-only; ADR-0113/0114 remain
Proposed/null; no device or execution authority was added.

### Cross-device plan §282 — Populated-Run ForgeRunObserved Flutter live E2E

The opt-in populated-Run Snaplink JWT → Go → Rust E2E projects the completed
Run summary through pure `ProjectRunObserved` and writes the content-free
`forge.run.observed.v1` value into a private 0600 Flutter input document. The
native Flutter test uses `ForgeRunObserved.fromJson`, `isFor`, and
`isDisplayOnly`, injects the selected value through `ForgeSessionsGate`, and
requires the metadata card. Local fake-API probes require foreign,
content-bearing, and authoritative candidates to remain hidden while the live
recorder sequence stays unchanged. No observer route, device or execution
authority, or Audit behavior was added; ADR-0039 remains planning-only and
ADR-0113/0114 remain Proposed/null.

### Cross-device plan §283 — Flutter selected-Run observer reader lifecycle

Snaplink Console's shared Web/App/Mobile Sessions surface accepts an explicit
`ForgeRunObservedReader` callback for one selected Conversation/Run. It
strictly re-decodes the content-free value, checks the selected binding and
all-false display boundary, refreshes it through the existing owner/session
refresh path, and keeps the last validated value visible during transient
refresh failure. The default Gate performs no observer request. Focused widget
coverage proves loading, binding, strict display-only validation, and refresh
replacement; ADR-0039 remains planning-only and ADR-0113/0114 remain
Proposed/null, with no device or execution authority added.

  Validation: final CLI unit tests 1,060/1,060 and independent review PASS; full acceptance is not claimed. The structural gate still reports 44 pre-existing violations outside this increment, and the formal run was rejected after candidate-journal watch coverage was lost.

- **IMPLEMENTED — Runtime local preview packaging**: Cargo-backed `--version` / `-V` and JSON version queries run without Hub effects. A pinned-toolchain, locked/offline Linux x86_64 packaging script generates a stripped binary, source/dependency manifest, validated archive, and checksums; it rejects output collisions and checks standalone reads, edits, command execution, persistence, and replay with local provider fixtures. CLI 1,067 unit + 3 integration tests, 14 Python regressions, 10 final release smoke checks, and independent review pass. Full repository formatting/structure/architecture/Clippy findings remain open; this is an unsigned local developer preview, not formal release acceptance or external publication. See `forge-runtime/PREVIEW.md`.
